      ?person pv:name ?name .
      FILTER (CONTAINS(?name, "Sabrina"))
    }

    in France?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    SELECT (COUNT(DISTINCT ?supplier) as ?result)
    WHERE
    {
      ?product pv:hasSupplier ?supplier .
      ?supplier pv:addressCountry "France" .
    }

      ck26:25-en
     
     
      
    Which coil has the greatest
    density?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?result
    WHERE
    {
      ?result pv:hasCategory <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/prod-cat-Coil> .
      ?result pv:width_mm ?width_mm .
      ?result pv:depth_mm ?depth_mm .
      ?result pv:height_mm ?height_mm .
      ?result pv:weight_g ?weight_g .
      BIND(?width_mm * ?depth_mm * ?height_mm / 1000000000 AS ?volume_m3)
      BIND(?weight_g / 1000 AS ?weight_kg)
      BIND(?weight_kg / ?volume_m3 AS ?density)
    }
    ORDER BY DESC(?density)
    LIMIT 1
    
         		
     
      
    {
        "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/hw-N832-1055352": 1
    }

    PREFIX dbd: <http://dbpedia.org/datatype/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbt: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Template:>
    PREFIX dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/>
    PREFIX dct: <http://purl.org/dc/terms/>
    PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>

      ?coil a pv:Hardware ;
            pv:name ?name ;
            pv:weight_g ?weight ;

    }
    ORDER BY DESC(?density)
    LIMIT 10

    Welche Spule hat die größte Dichte?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?result
    WHERE
    {
      ?result pv:hasCategory <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/prod-cat-Coil> .
      ?result pv:width_mm ?width_mm .
      ?result pv:depth_mm ?depth_mm .
      ?result pv:height_mm ?height_mm .
      ?result pv:weight_g ?weight_g .
      BIND(?width_mm * ?depth_mm * ?height_mm / 1000000000 AS ?volume_m3)
      BIND(?weight_g / 1000 AS ?weight_kg)
      BIND(?weight_kg / ?volume_m3 AS ?density)
    }
    ORDER BY DESC(?density)
    LIMIT 1
    
         		
     
      
    {
        "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/hw-N832-1055352": 1
    }
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX dbc: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Category:>
    PREFIX dbd: <http://dbpedia.org/datatype/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbt: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Template:>
    PREFIX dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/>
    PREFIX dct: <http://purl.org/dc/terms/>
    PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>
    PREFIX owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#>

    PREFIX rdfa: <http://www.w3.org/ns/rdfa#>

    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    PREFIX ecc: <https://ns.eccenca.com/>
    PREFIX void: <http://rdfs.org/ns/void#>
    PREFIX vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/>
    SELECT ?p ?name ?density WHERE {
      ?p a pv:Hardware ;
         pv:name ?name ;
         pv:weight_g ?weight ;
         pv:height_mm ?h ;
         pv:width_mm ?w ;
         pv:depth_mm ?d .
      FILTER(CONTAINS(LCASE(STR(?name)), "coil"))
      BIND(xsd:decimal(?weight) / (xsd:decimal(?h) * xsd:decimal(?w) * xsd:decimal(?d)) AS ?density)
    }
    ORDER BY DESC(?density)
    LIMIT 1

    directory that includes the name,
    email, and phone number of every
    employee who doesn’t manage anyone,
    sorted alphabetically by name?

    SELECT ?empl ?name ?email ?phone
    WHERE {
      ?emplClass rdfs:subClassOf* pv:Employee .
      ?empl a ?emplClass ;
          pv:name ?name ;
          pv:email ?email .
      OPTIONAL { ?empl pv:phone ?phone }
      FILTER NOT EXISTS { [] pv:hasManager ?empl . }
    }
    ORDER BY ?name

    SELECT ?employee ?name ?email ?phone WHERE {
      ?employee a pv:Employee ;
                pv:name ?name ;
                pv:email ?email ;
                pv:phone ?phone .
      FILTER NOT EXISTS { ?subordinate pv:hasManager ?employee }
    } ORDER BY ?name LIMIT 10

      ck26:27-de
     
     
      
    Könnten Sie ein Telefonverzeichnis
    für die Mitarbeiter erstellen, das
    den Namen, die E-Mail-Adresse und
    die Telefonnummer aller Mitarbeiter
    enthält, die keine leitende
    Position innehaben, und dieses
    alphabetisch nach Namen sortieren?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?empl ?name ?email ?phone
    WHERE {
      ?emplClass rdfs:subClassOf* pv:Employee .
      ?empl a ?emplClass ;
          pv:name ?name ;
          pv:email ?email .
      OPTIONAL { ?empl pv:phone ?phone }
      FILTER NOT EXISTS { [] pv:hasManager ?empl . }
    }
    ORDER BY ?name

      ck26:28-en
     
     
      
    Is there a service that can handle
    a BOM containing parts sourced from
    Russia?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    ASK
    WHERE {
      { # all BOMs with parts from russia
        SELECT ?bom
        WHERE {
          ?bom pv:hasBomPart ?bomPart .
          ?bomPart pv:hasPart ?hw .
          ?hw pv:hasSupplier ?suppl .
          ?suppl pv:addressCountry ?country .
          FILTER (CONTAINS(LCASE(?country), LCASE("Russia")))
        }
      }

      # services eligible for parts associated with these BOMs?
      ?s a pv:Service .
      ?s pv:eligibleFor ?hw .
      ?bom pv:hasBomPart ?bomPart .
      ?bomPart pv:hasPart ?hw .
    }
    
         		
     
      
    {
        "true": 1
    }

        pv:name ?name .

    SELECT ?cat ?lightest ?heaviest WHERE {
      {
        SELECT ?cat (SAMPLE(?l) AS ?lightest) (SAMPLE(?h) AS ?heaviest) WHERE {
          {
            SELECT ?cat (MIN(?w) AS ?minW) (MAX(?w) AS ?maxW) WHERE {
              ?item a pv:Hardware ; pv:hasCategory ?cat ; pv:weight_g ?w .
            }
            GROUP BY ?cat
          }
          ?l a pv:Hardware ; pv:hasCategory ?cat ; pv:weight_g ?minW .
          ?h a pv:Hardware ; pv:hasCategory ?cat ; pv:weight_g ?maxW .
        }
        GROUP BY ?cat
      }
    }
    ORDER BY ?cat
    LIMIT 50

    Für jede Produktkategorie, welches
    Hardware-Produkt ist das leichteste
    und welches das schwerste?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?cat (MIN(?w) AS ?minWeight) (MAX(?w) AS ?maxWeight)
    WHERE {
      ?catItem a pv:ProductCategory ;
        pv:name ?cat .
      ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
        pv:hasCategory ?catItem ;
        pv:weight_g ?w .
    }
    GROUP BY ?cat

    SELECT ?cat
           (GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT STR(?lightest); separator=", ") AS ?lightestProducts)
           (GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT STR(?heaviest); separator=", ") AS ?heaviestProducts)
    WHERE {
      {
        SELECT ?cat (MIN(?w) AS ?minW) (MAX(?w) AS ?maxW) WHERE {
          ?prod pv:hasCategory ?cat ; pv:weight_g ?w .
          FILTER(STRSTARTS(STR(?prod), "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/hw-"))
        } GROUP BY ?cat
      }
      {
        SELECT DISTINCT ?cat ?lightest WHERE {
          {
            SELECT ?cat (MIN(?w) AS ?minW) WHERE {
              ?prod pv:hasCategory ?cat ; pv:weight_g ?w .
              FILTER(STRSTARTS(STR(?prod), "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/hw-"))
            } GROUP BY ?cat
          }
          ?lightest pv:hasCategory ?cat ; pv:weight_g ?minW .
          FILTER(STRSTARTS(STR(?lightest), "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/hw-"))
        }
      }
      {
        SELECT DISTINCT ?cat ?heaviest WHERE {
          {
            SELECT ?cat (MAX(?w) AS ?maxW) WHERE {
              ?prod pv:hasCategory ?cat ; pv:weight_g ?w .
              FILTER(STRSTARTS(STR(?prod), "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/hw-"))
            } GROUP BY ?cat
          }
          ?heaviest pv:hasCategory ?cat ; pv:weight_g ?maxW .
          FILTER(STRSTARTS(STR(?heaviest), "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/hw-"))
        }
      }
    }
    GROUP BY ?cat
    LIMIT 10

      ck26:32-en
     
     
      
    What is the average price in euros
    of each supplier's products,
    rounded to a reasonable amount?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?name (ROUND(AVG(?amt) * 100) / 100 AS ?avgEur) WHERE {
      ?sup a pv:Supplier ;
        pv:name ?name .
      ?hw pv:hasSupplier ?sup ;
        pv:price/pv:amount ?amt ;
        pv:price/pv:currency "EUR" .
    }
    GROUP BY ?sup ?name

    PREFIX dbc: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Category:>
    PREFIX dbd: <http://dbpedia.org/datatype/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbt: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Template:>
    PREFIX dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/>
    PREFIX dct: <http://purl.org/dc/terms/>
    PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>
    PREFIX owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    PREFIX rdfa: <http://www.w3.org/ns/rdfa#>
    PREFIX rdfdf: <http://www.openlinksw.com/virtrdf-data-formats#>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    PREFIX ecc: <https://ns.eccenca.com/>
    PREFIX void: <http://rdfs.org/ns/void#>
    PREFIX vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/>
    SELECT ?supplier (ROUND(AVG(xsd:decimal(?amount)) * 100) / 100 AS ?avgPriceEUR) WHERE {
      ?product pv:hasSupplier ?supplier ;
               pv:price ?price .
      ?price pv:amount ?amount ;
             pv:currency "EUR" .
    }
    GROUP BY ?supplier
    ORDER BY ?supplier

    Was ist der durchschnittliche Preis
    in Euro für die Produkte jedes
    Anbieters, gerundet auf einen
    angemessenen Betrag?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?name (ROUND(AVG(?amt) * 100) / 100 AS ?avgEur) WHERE {
      ?sup a pv:Supplier ;
        pv:name ?name .
      ?hw pv:hasSupplier ?sup ;
        pv:price/pv:amount ?amt ;
        pv:price/pv:currency "EUR" .
    }
    GROUP BY ?sup ?name

    PREFIX dbc: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Category:>
    PREFIX dbd: <http://dbpedia.org/datatype/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbt: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Template:>
    PREFIX dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/>
    PREFIX dct: <http://purl.org/dc/terms/>
    PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>
    PREFIX owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    PREFIX rdfa: <http://www.w3.org/ns/rdfa#>
    PREFIX rdfdf: <http://www.openlinksw.com/virtrdf-data-formats#>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    PREFIX ecc: <https://ns.eccenca.com/>
    PREFIX void: <http://rdfs.org/ns/void#>
    PREFIX vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/>
    SELECT ?supplier (ROUND(AVG(xsd:decimal(?amount)) * 100) / 100 AS ?avgPriceEUR)
    WHERE {
      ?product pv:hasSupplier ?supplier ;
               pv:price ?price .
      ?price pv:amount ?amount ;
             pv:currency "EUR" .
    }
    GROUP BY ?supplier
    ORDER BY ?supplier

    Do any departments lack an assigned
    manager?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>

    ASK
    WHERE {
      ?dept a pv:Department .
      FILTER NOT EXISTS {
        ?m pv:memberOf ?dept .
        ?m a pv:Manager .
      }
    }
    
         		
     
      
    {
        "true": 0
    }
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX dbc: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Category:>
    PREFIX dbd: <http://dbpedia.org/datatype/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbt: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Template:>
    PREFIX dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/>
    PREFIX dct: <http://purl.org/dc/terms/>
    PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>
    PREFIX owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    PREFIX rdfa: <http://www.w3.org/ns/rdfa#>
    PREFIX rdfdf: <http://www.openlinksw.com/virtrdf-data-formats#>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    PREFIX ecc: <https://ns.eccenca.com/>
    PREFIX void: <http://rdfs.org/ns/void#>
    PREFIX vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/>
    SELECT (COUNT(?dept) AS ?cnt) WHERE {
      ?dept a pv:Department .
      FILTER NOT EXISTS {
        ?mgr a pv:Manager ;
             pv:memberOf ?dept .
      }
    }

                pv:name ?name ;

    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?name ?locality ?ccode ?country
    WHERE {
      ?sup a pv:Supplier ;
        pv:name ?name .
      OPTIONAL { ?sup pv:addressLocality ?locality . }
      OPTIONAL { ?sup pv:addressCountryCode ?ccode . }
      OPTIONAL { ?sup pv:addressCountry ?country . }
    }

    PREFIX ecc: <https://ns.eccenca.com/>
    PREFIX void: <http://rdfs.org/ns/void#>
    PREFIX vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?supplier ?name ?countryObj ?countryLabel WHERE {
      ?supplier a pv:Supplier ;
                pv:name ?name .
      OPTIONAL { 
        ?supplier pv:country ?countryObj .
        OPTIONAL { ?countryObj rdfs:label ?countryLabel }
      }
    }
    LIMIT 10

      ck26:35-en
     
     
      
    For each product, could you list
    the other products it's compatible
    with and show the price differences
    between them?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv:    <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?prod ?compatible (?prodAmount - ?compatibleAmount AS ?priceDiff)
    WHERE {
      ?prodClass rdfs:subClassOf* pv:Product .
      ?prod a ?prodClass ;
        pv:compatibleProduct ?compatible .
      ?prod pv:price/pv:amount ?prodAmount ;
        pv:price/pv:currency "EUR" .
      ?compatible pv:price/pv:amount ?compatibleAmount ;
        pv:price/pv:currency "EUR" .
    }

      ck26:35-de
     
     
      
    Könnten Sie für jedes Produkt
    auflisten, mit welchen anderen
    Produkten es kompatibel ist, und
    die Preisunterschiede zwischen
    diesen Produkten anzeigen?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv:    <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?prod ?compatible (?prodAmount - ?compatibleAmount AS ?priceDiff)
    WHERE {
      ?prodClass rdfs:subClassOf* pv:Product .
      ?prod a ?prodClass ;
        pv:compatibleProduct ?compatible .
      ?prod pv:price/pv:amount ?prodAmount ;
        pv:price/pv:currency "EUR" .
      ?compatible pv:price/pv:amount ?compatibleAmount ;
        pv:price/pv:currency "EUR" .
    }

    relevant, and what are the top
    three associated skills?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?cat ?name
    WHERE {
      ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
        pv:hasCategory ?cat .
      ?cat pv:name ?name .
    }
    GROUP BY ?cat ?name
    ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(*))
    LIMIT 3

    SELECT ?term (COUNT(*) AS ?freq) WHERE {
      ?product pv:hasCategory <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/prod-cat-Compensator> .
      ?product rdfs:label ?label .
      VALUES ?term { "Compensator" "Transistor" "Film" "Memristor" "Resonator" }

    }
    GROUP BY ?term
    ORDER BY DESC(?freq)

    WHERE {
      {
        SELECT ?topCat
        WHERE {
          ?agent pv:areaOfExpertise ?topCat .
        }
        GROUP BY ?topCat
        ORDER BY DESC(COUNT(?agent))
        LIMIT 1
      }
      ?agent pv:areaOfExpertise ?topCat ;
             pv:areaOfExpertise ?relatedExpertise .
      FILTER(?relatedExpertise != ?topCat)
    }
    LIMIT 20

    For each bill of materials, show
    the count of parts and the total
    material quantity, but display only
    those BOMs with more than 600
    items, sorted in descending order.
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?bom (COUNT(?part) AS ?partCount) (SUM(?qty) AS ?totalQty)
    WHERE {
      ?bom a pv:BillOfMaterial ;
        pv:hasBomPart ?part .
      ?part pv:quantity ?qty_ .
      BIND(xsd:int(?qty_) AS ?qty)
    }
    GROUP BY ?bom
    HAVING (SUM(?qty) > 600)
    ORDER BY DESC(SUM(?qty))

      OPTIONAL { ?empl pv:hasManager/pv:name ?manager . }

    Könnten Sie mir bitte eine
    aktualisierte Kontaktliste zukommen
    lassen, die den Namen, die E-Mail-
    Adresse, die Telefonnummer, die
    Abteilung und den direkten
    Vorgesetzten jedes Mitarbeiters
    enthält?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?name ?email ?phone ?dept ?manager
    WHERE {
      ?agentClass rdfs:subClassOf* pv:Agent .
      ?empl a ?agentClass ;
        pv:name ?name .
      OPTIONAL { ?empl pv:email ?email . }
      OPTIONAL { ?empl pv:phone ?phone . }
      OPTIONAL { ?empl pv:memberOf/pv:name ?dept . }
      OPTIONAL { ?empl pv:hasManager/pv:name ?manager . }
    }

    Can you list the dimensions of
    hardware items that are wider than
    they are tall and have a depth
    under 50 mm?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?hw ?width ?height ?depth
    WHERE {
      ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
        pv:width_mm ?width ;
        pv:height_mm ?height ;
        pv:depth_mm ?depth .
      FILTER (?width > ?height && ?depth < 50)
    }

      ck26:39-de
     
     
      
    Können Sie die Abmessungen von
    Hardware-Artikeln auflisten, die
    breiter als hoch sind und eine
    Tiefe von unter 50 mm haben?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?hw ?width ?height ?depth
    WHERE {
      ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
        pv:width_mm ?width ;
        pv:height_mm ?height ;
        pv:depth_mm ?depth .
      FILTER (?width > ?height && ?depth < 50)
    }

    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    WHERE {

      ?m pv:name ?name .

          ?e pv:hasManager ?m .

          ?m pv:memberOf ?dept .

      ck26:42-en
     
     
      
    Which bill of materials has the
    highest average unit cost for its
    hardware components, and what is
    that average?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
    PREFIX prodi: <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?bom (ROUND(AVG(?spst / ?sq) * 100) / 100 AS ?avgUnitCost)
    WHERE {
      {
        SELECT ?bom (SUM(?partSubTotal) AS ?spst) (SUM(xsd:int(?quant)) AS ?sq)
        WHERE {
          ?bom a pv:BillOfMaterial ;
            pv:hasBomPart ?part .
          ?part pv:quantity ?quant .
          ?part pv:hasPart/pv:price/pv:amount ?amount .
          ?part pv:hasPart/pv:price/pv:currency "EUR" .
          BIND(xsd:int(?quant) * ?amount AS ?partSubTotal)
        }
        GROUP BY ?bom
      }
    }
    GROUP BY ?bom
    ORDER BY DESC(ROUND(AVG(?spst / ?sq) * 100) / 100)
    LIMIT 1

    Könnten Sie Beispiele für
    Produktkompatibilitätszyklen nennen
    - Fälle, in denen Produkt A angibt,
    dass es mit Produkt B kompatibel
    ist, und Produkt B wiederum Produkt
    A als kompatibel auflistet?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?a ?b
    WHERE {
      ?a pv:compatibleProduct ?b .
      FILTER (STR(?a) < STR(?b))
    }

      ck26:44-en
     
     
      
    Which hardware items fall within
    the top 10% of widths, and what are
    their IDs, names, and width
    measurements?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?id ?name ?width
    WHERE {
      { # get min-max range
        SELECT (MIN(?width) AS ?minW) (MAX(?width) AS ?maxW)
        WHERE {
          ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
            pv:width_mm ?width .
        }
      }

      # get 90. percentile
      BIND(?minW + (0.9 * (?maxW - ?minW)) AS ?pct90)

      ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
        pv:id ?id ;
        pv:name ?name ;
        pv:width_mm ?width .

      FILTER(?width >= ?pct90)
    }
    ORDER BY DESC(?width)

      ck26:44-de
     
     
      
    Welche Hardware-Komponenten gehören
    zu den 10% mit der größten Breite,
    und welche IDs, Namen und
    Breitenmaße haben sie?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    SELECT ?id ?name ?width
    WHERE {
      { # get min-max range
        SELECT (MIN(?width) AS ?minW) (MAX(?width) AS ?maxW)
        WHERE {
          ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
            pv:width_mm ?width .
        }
      }

      # get 90. percentile
      BIND(?minW + (0.9 * (?maxW - ?minW)) AS ?pct90)

      ?hw a pv:Hardware ;
        pv:id ?id ;
        pv:name ?name ;
        pv:width_mm ?width .

      FILTER(?width >= ?pct90)
    }
    ORDER BY DESC(?width)

    How many suppliers can provide
    alternative compatible products for
    the K367 Strain Encoder?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    SELECT DISTINCT (COUNT(?supplier) AS ?result)
    WHERE
    {
      <http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/hw-K367-1320550> pv:compatibleProduct ?alternative .
      ?alternative pv:hasSupplier ?supplier .
    }
    
         		
     
      
    {
        "6": 1
    }

      ck26:50-en
     
     
      
    Which department is responsible for
    the most products, and how many
    products is that?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?result (COUNT(?result) AS ?count)
    WHERE
    {
      ?result pv:responsibleFor ?product .
    }
    GROUP BY ?result
    ORDER BY DESC(?count)
    LIMIT 1
    
         		
     
      
    {
        "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/dept-85880": 1,
        "12": 1
    }
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX dbc: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Category:>
    PREFIX dbd: <http://dbpedia.org/datatype/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbt: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Template:>
    PREFIX dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/>
    PREFIX dct: <http://purl.org/dc/terms/>
    PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>
    PREFIX owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    PREFIX rdfa: <http://www.w3.org/ns/rdfa#>
    PREFIX rdfdf: <http://www.openlinksw.com/virtrdf-data-formats#>
    PREFIX rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#>
    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    PREFIX ecc: <https://ns.eccenca.com/>
    PREFIX void: <http://rdfs.org/ns/void#>
    PREFIX vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/>
    SELECT ?dept ?count
    WHERE {
      {
        SELECT ?dept (COUNT(?product) AS ?count)
        WHERE {
          ?dept a pv:Department ;
                pv:responsibleFor ?product .
        }
        GROUP BY ?dept
      }
      {
        SELECT (MAX(?c) AS ?maxCount)
        WHERE {
          {
            SELECT ?dept (COUNT(?product) AS ?c)
            WHERE {
              ?dept a pv:Department ;
                    pv:responsibleFor ?product .
            }
            GROUP BY ?dept
          }
        }
      }
      FILTER(?count = ?maxCount)
    }
    
         		
     
      
    {
        "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/dept-85880": 1,
        "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/dept-22183": 1,
        "12": 1
    }
    
         		
     
      
    {
        "set_P": 0.6666666666666666,
        "set_recall": 1.0,
        "set_F": 0.8
    }

    Welche Abteilung ist für die
    meisten Produkte verantwortlich,
    und wie viele Produkte sind das?
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>
    SELECT DISTINCT ?result (COUNT(?result) AS ?count)
    WHERE
    {
      ?result pv:responsibleFor ?product .
    }
    GROUP BY ?result
    ORDER BY DESC(?count)
    LIMIT 1
    
         		
     
      
    {
        "http://ld.company.org/prod-instances/dept-85880": 1,
        "12": 1
    }
    
         		
     
      
    PREFIX dbc: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Category:>
    PREFIX dbd: <http://dbpedia.org/datatype/>
    PREFIX dbo: <http://dbpedia.org/ontology/>
    PREFIX dbp: <http://dbpedia.org/property/>
    PREFIX dbr: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/>
    PREFIX dbt: <http://dbpedia.org/resource/Template:>
    PREFIX dc: <http://purl.org/dc/elements/1.1/>
    PREFIX dct: <http://purl.org/dc/terms/>
    PREFIX foaf: <http://xmlns.com/foaf/0.1/>
    PREFIX owl: <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#>
    PREFIX rdf: <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#>
    PREFIX rdfa: <http://www.w3.org/ns/rdfa#>
    PREFIX rdfdf: <http://www.openlinksw.com/virtrdf-data-formats#>

    PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
    PREFIX pv: <http://ld.company.org/prod-vocab/>

    PREFIX void: <http://rdfs.org/ns/void#>
    PREFIX vann: <http://purl.org/vocab/vann/>
    SELECT ?department (COUNT(?product) AS ?productCount) WHERE {
      ?department a pv:Department ;
                  pv:responsibleFor ?product .
    }
    GROUP BY ?department
    ORDER BY DESC(?productCount) ?department
    LIMIT 1
